```python
"""
Hybrid non-convex optimizer for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.

Main ingredients:
- FFT-based objective evaluation.
- Diverse analytical/Fourier/parent initializations.
- Multiplicative mirror descent and projected gradient descent
  using top-peak softmax/top-k subgradients.
- LP-based majorization updates:
      1) maximize sum(g) subject to a*g <= max(a*a), g>=0
      2) minimize a linearized peak model on the simplex
- Fast coordinate-transfer local polish with in-place convolution updates.
- Final validation using the provided evaluate_sequence() if available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

try:
    from operator import itemgetter
except Exception:
    itemgetter = None

FFT_CACHE = {}


def get_fft_size(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    if m in FFT_CACHE:
        return FFT_CACHE[m]
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    FFT_CACHE[m] = p
    return p


def normalize_sequence(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(arr.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(arr.size)
    return arr / s


def convolve_full(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0]) * float(a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = get_fft_size(size)
    af = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    cf = af * af
    c = np.fft.irfft(cf, L).real[:size]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def sequence_score(a):
    arr = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(arr.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = convolve_full(arr)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    cond = u * np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64) > (cssv - 1.0)
    rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0]
    if rho.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    rho = int(rho[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = out.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return out / s


def active_subgradient(conv, a, top_k=32):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    conv = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(conv.size)
    sg = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return sg

    k = int(top_k)
    if k < 1:
        k = 1
    if k > m:
        k = m

    if k == m:
        idx = np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64)
        w = np.maximum(conv, 0.0)
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw < 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
        else:
            w = w / sw
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(-conv, k - 1)[:k].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
        w = np.maximum(conv[idx], 0.0)
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw < 1e-300:
            w = np.ones(k, dtype=np.float64) / float(k)
        else:
            w2 = w * w
            s2 = float(w2.sum())
            if s2 > 1e-300:
                w = w2 / s2
            else:
                w = w / sw

    for ii in range(len(idx)):
        kk = int(idx[ii])
        ww = float(w[ii])
        if ww <= 0.0:
            continue
        lo = kk - n + 1
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = kk
        if hi >= n:
            hi = n - 1
        if lo > hi:
            continue
        start = kk - lo
        stop = kk - hi - 1
        seg = a[start:stop:-1]
        sg[lo:hi + 1] += ww * seg

    sg -= float(np.mean(sg))
    return sg


def mirror_descent(a0, time_limit, eta_init=0.8, top_k=32, max_pat=40):
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sequence(a0), float("inf")

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
    a /= a.sum()
    n = int(a.size)
    conv = convolve_full(a)
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv))
    best = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    eta = float(eta_init)
    patience = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        sg = active_subgradient(conv, a, top_k)
        sg = sg - float(np.mean(sg))
        scale = float(np.abs(sg).max())
        if scale < 1e-18:
            break
        sg = sg / scale

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            lf = -eta * float(mult) * sg
            lf = np.clip(lf, -50.0, 50.0)
            lf -= float(np.max(lf))
            na = a * np.exp(lf)
            na = np.maximum(na, 1e-15)
            ss = float(na.sum())
            if ss <= 1e-15:
                continue
            na = na / ss
            nc = convolve_full(na)
            no = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(nc))
            if no <= obj * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12:
                a = na
                conv = nc
                obj = no
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            patience += 1
            eta *= 0.55
            if patience >= max_pat or eta < 1e-7:
                break
        else:
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj
                best = a.copy()
                patience = 0
            else:
                patience += 1
                if patience >= max_pat:
                    break

    return best, best_obj


def pgd(a0, time_limit, lr_init=0.15, top_k=32, max_pat=40):
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sequence(a0), float("inf")

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
    a /= a.sum()
    n = int(a.size)
    conv = convolve_full(a)
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv))
    best = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    lr = float(lr_init)
    patience = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        sg = active_subgradient(conv, a, top_k)
        sg = sg - float(np.mean(sg))
        scale = float(np.abs(sg).max())
        if scale < 1e-18:
            break
        sg = sg / scale

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            na = a - lr * float(mult) * sg
            na = project_simplex(na)
            na = np.maximum(na, 1e-15)
            ss = na.sum()
            if ss <= 1e-15:
                continue
            na = na / ss
            nc = convolve_full(na)
            no = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(nc))
            if no <= obj * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12:
                a = na
                conv = nc
                obj = no
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            patience += 1
            lr *= 0.55
            if patience >= max_pat or lr < 1e-7:
                break
        else:
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
                best_obj = obj
                best = a.copy()
                patience = 0
            else:
                patience += 1
                if patience >= max_pat:
                    break

    return best, best_obj


def build_conv_matrix(a, extra_col=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        if extra_col is None:
            return np.zeros((0, 0), dtype=np.float64)
        return np.zeros((0, 1), dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if extra_col is None:
        A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        A = np.zeros((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a
    if extra_col is not None:
        A[:, n] = float(extra_col)
    return A


def run_linprog(c, A_ub, b_ub, bounds, A_eq=None, b_eq=None, time_limit_seconds=8.0):
    if linprog is None:
        return None

    opts = {"time_limit": max(1.0, float(time_limit_seconds))}
    try:
        return linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            method="highs",
            options=opts
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        return linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            method="highs"
        )
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        return linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq
        )
    except Exception:
        return None


def lp_linearized_update(a, conv, time_limit_seconds=8.0):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n > 600:
        return None

    if conv is None:
        conv = convolve_full(a)
    D = np.asarray(conv, dtype=np.float64)
    dmax = float(np.max(D))
    if dmax <= 1e-18:
        return a

    A = build_conv_matrix(a, -0.5)
    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[-1] = 1.0
    rhs = 0.5 * D

    zscale = max(1.0, dmax)
    zlb = -10.0 * dmax - 1e-8 * zscale
    zub = 10.0 * dmax + 1e-8 * zscale

    bounds = [(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)]
    bounds.append((zlb, zub))

    Aeq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    Aeq[0, :n] = 1.0
    beq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    res = run_linprog(c, A, rhs, bounds, A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=beq, time_limit_seconds=time_limit_seconds)
    x = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
    if x is None:
        return None
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size < n:
        return None
    b = x[:n]
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return b / s


def lp_max_sum_update(a, M, time_limit_seconds=8.0):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n > 600:
        return None
    M = float(M)
    if M <= 1e-18:
        return a

    A = build_conv_matrix(a)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None) for _ in range(n)]

    res = run_linprog(c, A, b, bounds, A_eq=None, b_eq=None, time_limit_seconds=time_limit_seconds)
    x = getattr(res, "x", None) if res is not None else None
    if x is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return g / s


def lp_improve_sequence(a0, time_limit, lp_time=8.0, use_linear=True, use_maxsum=True):
    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    conv = convolve_full(a)
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv))
    best = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return best, best_obj

    if linprog is None or n > 600:
        while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
            rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if rem <= 1.0:
                break
            alloc = min(rem, 300.0)
            a1, o1 = mirror_descent(
                a,
                alloc * 0.55,
                eta_init=0.75,
                top_k=max(16, min(64, n // 4))
            )
            if o1 < best_obj:
                best_obj = o1
                best = a1.copy()
                a = a1

            rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if rem <= 1.0:
                break
            alloc = min(rem, 250.0)
            a2, o2 = pgd(
                a,
                alloc * 0.6,
                lr_init=0.12,
                top_k=max(16, min(64, n // 4))
            )
            if o2 < best_obj:
                best_obj = o2
                best = a2.copy()
                a = a2

            rem = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
            if rem <= 1.0:
                break
            alloc = min(rem, 200.0)
            a3, o3 = mirror_descent(
                a,
                alloc,
                eta_init=0.35,
                top_k=max(12, min(48, n // 5))
            )
            if o3 < best_obj:
                best_obj = o3
                best = a3.copy()
                a = a3
        return best, best_obj

    modes = []
    if use_linear:
        modes.append(0)
    if use_maxsum:
        modes.append(1)
    if not modes:
        return best, best_obj

    mix_values = (0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0)
    it = 0
    stall = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        rem_all = time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        if rem_all <= 0.5:
            break
        tl = min(float(lp_time), max(1.0, rem_all))
        M = float(np.max(conv))
        mode = modes[it % len(modes)]
        it += 1

        base = None
        if mode == 0:
            base = lp_linearized_update(a, conv, tl)
        else:
            base = lp_max_sum_update(a, M * (1.0 + 1e-8), tl)

        if base is None:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 4:
                break
            continue

        best_step = None
        best_step_obj = obj

        for mm in mix_values:
            if mm >= 1.0:
                cand = base.copy()
            else:
                cand = (1.0 - float(mm)) * a + float(mm) * base
            cand = normalize_sequence(cand)
            cc = convolve_full(cand)
            oo = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(cc))
            if oo < best_step_obj - 1e-13:
                best_step_obj = oo
                best_step = cand

        if best_step is None:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 4:
                break
            continue

        a = best_step
        conv = convolve_full(a)
        obj = best_step_obj

        if obj < best_obj - 1e-12:
            best_obj = obj
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 5:
                break

    return best, best_obj


def apply_move_inplace(conv, a, p, q, delta):
    if delta == 0.0 or p == q:
        return
    n = int(a.size)
    conv[p:p + n] -= 2.0 * delta * a
    conv[q:q + n] += 2.0 * delta * a
    conv[p + p] += delta * delta
    conv[q + q] += delta * delta
    conv[p + q] -= 2.0 * delta * delta


def local_polish(a0, time_limit, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(20260101)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        return normalize_sequence(a0)

    a = normalize_sequence(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2:
        return a

    conv = convolve_full(a)
    sum_a = float(a.sum())
    sum_sq = sum_a * sum_a if sum_a > 1e-300 else 1.0
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv)) / sum_sq
    best = a.copy()
    best_obj = obj

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    recompute_count = 0
    no_improve = 0
    top_k = max(8, min(28, n // 10))

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(time_limit, 1.0)))
        T = 3e-5 * (1.0 - frac) ** 2 + 1e-7

        sg = active_subgradient(conv, a, top_k)
        src = np.maximum(sg, 0.0) * a
        src = np.where(np.isfinite(src), src, 0.0)
        src += 1e-14
        ss = float(src.sum())
        if ss <= 1e-15:
            src = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
            ss = float(src.sum())
            if ss <= 1e-15:
                src = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
            else:
                src = src / ss

        cnt = min(20, n)
        if cnt < n:
            src_idx = np.argpartition(-src, cnt - 1)[:cnt].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
        else:
            src_idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)

        p = int(src_idx[rng.integers(src_idx.size)])
        while a[p] <= 1e-14 and src_idx.size > 1:
            p = int(src_idx[rng.integers(src_idx.size)])
        if a[p] <= 1e-14:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 500:
                a = normalize_sequence(a + 0.03 * rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
                conv = convolve_full(a)
                sum_a = float(a.sum())
                sum_sq = sum_a * sum_a if sum_a > 1e-300 else 1.0
                obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv)) / sum_sq
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        tgt = 1.0 + 2.0 * np.maximum(-sg, 0.0)
        tgt = np.where(np.isfinite(tgt), tgt, 0.0)
        tgt[p] = 0.0
        ts = float(tgt.sum())
        if ts <= 1e-15:
            tgt = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            tgt[p] = 0.0
            ts = float(tgt.sum())
        if ts <= 1e-15:
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        tgt = tgt / ts

        cnt = min(24, n - 1)
        if cnt <= 0:
            no_improve += 1
            continue
        tgt_idx = np.argpartition(-tgt, cnt - 1)[:cnt].astype(np.int64, copy=False)
        valid = np.setdiff1d(tgt_idx, np.array([p], dtype=np.int64))
        if valid.size == 0:
            valid = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
            valid = np.delete(valid, p)
        q = int(valid[rng.integers(valid.size)])

        base_delta = float(a[p])
        best_delta = 0.0
        best_new_obj = obj

        for fmove in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
            d = base_delta * float(fmove)
            trial = conv.copy()
            apply_move_inplace(trial, a, p, q, d)
            no = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(trial)) / sum_sq
            if no < best_new_obj:
                best_new_obj = no
                best_delta = d

        if best_delta > 0.0:
            apply_move_inplace(conv, a, p, q, best_delta)
            a[p] = max(0.0, a[p] - best_delta)
            a[q] += best_delta
            obj = best_new_obj
            recompute_count += 1
            no_improve = 0
            if obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
                best_obj = obj
                best = a.copy()
        else:
            d = base_delta * float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.35))
            trial = conv.copy()
            apply_move_inplace(trial, a, p, q, d)
            no = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(trial)) / sum_sq
            delta_obj = no - obj
            accept = False
            if delta_obj <= 1e-12:
                accept = True
            elif delta_obj < 50.0 * T:
                accept = rng.random() < math.exp(-delta_obj / max(T, 1e-30))
            if accept:
                apply_move_inplace(conv, a, p, q, d)
                a[p] = max(0.0, a[p] - d)
                a[q] += d
                obj = no
                recompute_count += 1
                if obj < best_obj - 1e-13:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_obj = obj
            else:
                no_improve += 1

        if recompute_count > 320:
            conv = convolve_full(a)
            sum_a = float(a.sum())
            sum_sq = sum_a * sum_a if sum_a > 1e-300 else 1.0
            obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv)) / sum_sq
            recompute_count = 0

        if no_improve > 5000:
            gamma = 0.05
            a = normalize_sequence((1.0 - gamma) * a + gamma * rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)))
            conv = convolve_full(a)
            sum_a = float(a.sum())
            sum_sq = sum_a * sum_a if sum_a > 1e-300 else 1.0
            obj = 2.0 * float(n) * float(np.max(conv)) / sum_sq
            no_improve = 0

    return best


def fourier_sqrt_init(n, rng, trials=5):
    if n <= 0 or trials <= 0:
        return None
    L = get_fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    h = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    h[:2 * n - 1] = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
    Ht = np.fft.rfft(h, L).astype(np.complex128)

    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for trial in range(int(trials)):
        F = np.sqrt(Ht)
        if trial > 0:
            signs = np.where(rng.random(F.size) < 0.5, -1.0, 1.0)
            if F.size > 0:
                signs[0] = 1.0
            F = F * signs

        g = np.fft.irfft(F, L)[:n]
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

        candidates = (np.maximum(g, 0.0), np.abs(g))
        for raw in candidates:
            s = float(raw.sum())
            if s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            cand = raw / s
            sc = sequence_score(cand)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best = cand.copy()

    return best


def fourier_phase_init(n, steps=18, rng=None):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(777)

    f = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    L = get_fft_size(2 * n - 1)
    h = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    h[:2 * n - 1] = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
    Ht = np.fft.rfft(h, L).astype(np.complex128)
    scale = max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(Ht))))

    for _ in range(int(steps)):
        F = np.fft.rfft(f, L)
        Hf = F * F
        absHf = np.abs(Hf)
        mask = absHf > 1e-12 * scale
        ratio = np.ones_like(Ht)
        if np.any(mask):
            ratio[mask] = Ht[mask] / Hf[mask]
        Fnew = F * np.sqrt(ratio)
        if not np.all(mask):
            Fnew[~mask] = F[~mask] + 0.1 * np.sqrt(Ht[~mask])

        g = np.fft.irfft(Fnew, L)[:n]
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)

        raw1 = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        s1 = float(raw1.sum())
        raw2 = np.abs(g)
        s2 = float(raw2.sum())

        if s1 >= s2 and s1 > 1e-12:
            f = raw1 / s1
        elif s2 > 1e-12:
            f = raw2 / s2
        else:
            break

    alt = np.abs(f)
    s = float(alt.sum())
    if s > 1e-12:
        alt /= s
        if sequence_score(alt) < sequence_score(f):
            f = alt
    return normalize_sequence(f)


def get_parent_sequence():
    p = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    if p is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return arr / s


def generate_initializations(n, parent, rng):
    inits = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    inits.append(("uniform", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)))

    if parent is not None:
        xp = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, parent.size, dtype=np.float64)
        fp = np.interp(x, xp, parent)
        inits.append(("parent", normalize_sequence(fp)))
        pnorm = normalize_sequence(fp)
        uni = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        inits.append(("parent_mix", normalize_sequence(0.8 * pnorm + 0.2 * uni)))

    for amp in (0.6, 1.5, 3.0):
        w = 1.0 + float(amp) * (4.0 * (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5))
        inits.append(("U" + str(amp), normalize_sequence(w)))

    for amp in (1.0, 3.0, 8.0):
        w = 1.0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + float(amp) * x)
        inits.append(("L" + str(amp), normalize_sequence(w)))
        w = 1.0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + float(amp) * (1.0 - x))
        inits.append(("R" + str(amp), normalize_sequence(w)))

    for amp in (0.6, 1.8):
        w = 1.0 + float(amp) * (1.0 - np.sin(np.pi * x))
        inits.append(("edge" + str(amp), normalize_sequence(w)))

    for r in range(2):
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for freq in (1, 2, 3, 5):
            amp = 0.4 * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / float(freq)))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            w = w + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * x + phase)
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-3)
        inits.append(("rand" + str(r), normalize_sequence(w)))

    f = fourier_sqrt_init(n, rng, trials=4)
    if f is not None:
        inits.append(("foursq", f))

    f = fourier_phase_init(n, steps=16, rng=rng)
    if f is not None:
        inits.append(("fourph", f))

    return inits


def add_to_top_list(score, a, top_list, max_keep=12):
    if a is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    top_list.append((float(score), normalize_sequence(a).copy()))
    while len(top_list) > int(max_keep):
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(top_list)):
            if top_list[i][0] > top_list[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        del top_list[worst]


def update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, a, tag=""):
    if a is None:
        return
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    score = sequence_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    add_to_top_list(score, a, top_list, 12)
    if score < best_holder[1]:
        best_holder[1] = score
        best_holder[0] = a.copy()
        print("  improved[%s]: score=%.9f n=%d" % (tag, score, a.size))


def safe_eval_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if float(a.sum()) < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")

    evals = globals().get("evaluate_sequence")
    if callable(evals):
        try:
            val = float(evals([float(v) for v in a]))
            if np.isfinite(val):
                return val
        except Exception:
            pass

    return sequence_score(a)


def scale_output(a):
    a = normalize_sequence(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = a * target
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a


def choose_actual_best(best_holder, top_list):
    candidates = []
    if best_holder[0] is not None:
        candidates.append(best_holder[0])
    for _, a in top_list:
        candidates.append(a)

    best_out = None
    best_val = float("inf")
    for a in candidates:
        out = scale_output(a)
        val = safe_eval_sequence(out)
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_out = out.copy()

    if best_out is None:
        best_out = scale_output(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))
    return best_out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    try:
        seed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        seed = 42
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0

    start = time.monotonic()
    if budget <= 35.0:
        return [1.0] * 64

    deadline = start + max(30.0, budget - 20.0)
    base_T = max(10.0, deadline - start)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = get_parent_sequence()

    best_holder = [None, float("inf")]
    top_list = []
    update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64) / 256.0, "init")

    lengths = [256, 512]
    if parent is not None:
        pl = int(parent.size)
        if 64 <= pl <= 2048 and pl not in lengths:
            lengths.append(pl)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))
    if not lengths:
        lengths = [256]

    phase1_end = start + min(400.0, max(5.0, 0.18 * base_T))
    phase2_end = start + min(max(10.0, base_T - 50.0), max(phase1_end + 5.0, 0.68 * base_T))
    phase3_end = start + min(max(10.0, base_T - 5.0), max(phase2_end + 5.0, 0.92 * base_T))
    phase1_end = max(start + 1.0, min(phase1_end, phase2_end - 2.0 if phase2_end > phase1_end + 3.0 else phase1_end))
    phase2_end = max(phase1_end + 1.0, min(phase2_end, phase3_end - 2.0 if phase3_end > phase2_end + 3.0 else phase2_end))
    phase3_end = max(phase2_end + 1.0, min(phase3_end, deadline - 1.0))

    print("Lengths to explore: %s" % (lengths,))

    # Phase 1: quick warm starts across lengths/diverse inits.
    print("Phase 1: quick warm starts...")
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= phase1_end:
            break
        inits = generate_initializations(n, parent, rng)
        inits = inits[:8]
        top_k = max(16, min(48, n // 4))
        for name, a0 in inits:
            if time.monotonic() >= phase1_end:
                break
            rem = phase1_end - time.monotonic()
            if rem <= 0.5:
                break
            cur = a0.copy()
            alloc1 = min(rem * 0.55, 25.0)
            alloc2 = min(rem * 0.35, 20.0)
            alloc3 = min(rem * 0.10, 8.0)

            if alloc1 > 0.4:
                cur, _ = mirror_descent(a0, alloc1, eta_init=0.9, top_k=top_k)
                update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "warm_" + name)

            if time.monotonic() >= phase1_end:
                break
            if alloc2 > 0.4:
                cur, _ = pgd(cur, alloc2, lr_init=0.15, top_k=top_k)
                update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "pgd_" + name)

            if time.monotonic() >= phase1_end:
                break
            if alloc3 > 0.4:
                cur, _ = mirror_descent(cur, alloc3, eta_init=0.45, top_k=max(12, top_k // 2))
                update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "warm2_" + name)

    if itemgetter is not None:
        top_list.sort(key=itemgetter(0))

    deep_cands = []
    for _, a in top_list:
        deep_cands.append(a)
        if len(deep_cands) >= 8:
            break
    if parent is not None:
        deep_cands.append(parent)
    if best_holder[0] is not None:
        deep_cands.append(best_holder[0])
    deep_cands = deep_cands[:10]

    # Phase 2: LP-majorization / fallback gradient deep optimization.
    print("Phase 2: LP / deep optimization on top candidates...")
    deep_count = max(1, len(deep_cands))
    for idx, a0 in enumerate(deep_cands):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase2_end:
            break
        rem = phase2_end - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(450.0, rem)
        if alloc < 4.0:
            break
        a0 = normalize_sequence(a0)
        nn = int(a0.size)
        lp_time = 5.0 if nn <= 300 else (8.0 if nn <= 512 else 10.0)
        print("  deep %d/%d n=%d budget=%.1fs" % (idx + 1, len(deep_cands), nn, alloc))
        ao, _ = lp_improve_sequence(
            a0,
            time_limit=alloc,
            lp_time=lp_time,
            use_linear=True,
            use_maxsum=True
        )
        update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, ao, "deep%d" % idx)

    if itemgetter is not None:
        top_list.sort(key=itemgetter(0))

    # Phase 3: local transfer polish.
    print("Phase 3: local polish...")
    polish_cands = []
    if best_holder[0] is not None:
        polish_cands.append(best_holder[0])
    for _, a in top_list:
        polish_cands.append(a)
        if len(polish_cands) >= 5:
            break
    polish_cands = polish_cands[:4]

    for idx, a0 in enumerate(polish_cands):
        if time.monotonic() >= phase3_end:
            break
        rem = phase3_end - time.monotonic()
        alloc = min(220.0, rem)
        if alloc < 2.0:
            break
        print("  polish %d/%d budget=%.1fs" % (idx + 1, len(polish_cands), alloc))
        ao = local_polish(a0, time_limit=alloc, rng=rng)
        update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, ao, "polish%d" % idx)

    # Phase 4: final polish.
    print("Phase 4: final polish...")
    if best_holder[0] is not None:
        final_end = deadline - 2.0
        cur = best_holder[0].copy()
        nn = int(cur.size)
        tok = max(16, min(48, nn // 4))
        rem = final_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 45.0:
            ao = local_polish(cur, time_limit=min(rem * 0.6, 180.0), rng=rng)
            sc = sequence_score(ao)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_holder[1]:
                cur = ao
            update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "final_local1")

        rem = final_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 25.0:
            cur, _ = mirror_descent(cur, time_limit=min(rem * 0.45, 80.0), eta_init=0.28, top_k=tok)
            update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "final_mirror")

        rem = final_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 15.0:
            cur, _ = pgd(cur, time_limit=min(rem * 0.45, 60.0), lr_init=0.05, top_k=tok)
            update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "final_pgd")

        rem = final_end - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 8.0:
            cur = local_polish(cur, time_limit=min(rem * 0.8, 45.0), rng=rng)
            update_best_state(best_holder, top_list, cur, "final_local2")

    final_a = choose_actual_best(best_holder, top_list)
    final_a = np.where(np.isfinite(final_a), final_a, 0.0)
    final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(final_a.sum())
    target = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(final_a.size))
    if s < 0.01:
        final_a = scale_output(np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64))
    else:
        final_a = final_a * (target / s)
        final_a = np.clip(final_a, 0.0, 1000.0)

    try:
        print("FINAL local score: %.9f" % sequence_score(final_a))
        print("FINAL actual score: %.9f" % safe_eval_sequence(final_a))
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(v) for v in final_a]
```
